Standard error is never compared.\n#\n#   sh sources/run.sh                    from the application root (how `drydock uat` runs it)\n#   sh uat/DrydockStats/bin/run.sh APP   from anywhere, grading the application root APP\n#\n# Exit 0 only when the build succeeds and every check passes.\nset -u\n\nHERE=$(cd \"$(dirname \"$0\")\" && pwd)\nAPP=$(cd \"${1:-.}\" && pwd)\nif [ -f \"$HERE/llm_sample.jsonl\" ]; then DATA=$HERE; else DATA=$(cd \"$HERE/../sources\" && pwd); fi\nWORK=$(mktemp -d)\ntrap 'rm -rf \"$WORK\"' EXIT INT TERM\nBIN=\"$WORK/ddkstat\"\nLOG=\"$DATA/llm_sample.jsonl\"\nMISSING=\"$WORK/no-such-log.jsonl\"\n\nif ! (cd \"$APP\" && go build -o \"$BIN\" ./cmd/ddkstat); then\n    echo \"error: go build ./cmd/ddkstat failed at the application root $APP.\" >&2\n    echo \"The deliverable is a Go module whose main package is ./cmd/ddkstat.\" >&2\n    exit 1\nfi\n\nPASSED=0\nFAILED=0\n\n# check <id> <expected exit> <ddkstat args...>  — expected standard output on stdin.\ncheck() {\n    id=$1\n    want_rc=$2\n    shift 2\n    cat > \"$WORK/want\"\n    (cd \"$APP\" && \"$BIN\" \"$@\") > \"$WORK/got\" 2> \"$WORK/err\"\n    rc=$?\n    if [ \"$rc\" -eq \"$want_rc\" ] && cmp -s \"$WORK/want\" \"$WORK/got\"; then\n        PASSED=$((PASSED + 1))\n        echo \"PASS $id: ddkstat $*\"\n    else\n        FAILED=$((FAILED + 1))\n        echo \"FAIL $id: ddkstat $*\"\n        echo \"  exit: expected $want_rc, got $rc\"\n        diff \"$WORK/want\" \"$WORK/got\" | sed 's/^/  /'\n        sed 's/^/  stderr: /' \"$WORK/err\"\n    fi\n}\n\n# ── A. The error is non-nil only when reading from r fails.\nfunc Read(r io.Reader, warn io.Writer) (Result, error) {\n\tif warn == nil {\n\t\twarn = io.Discard\n\t}\n\tvar res Result\n\tbr := errors.Is(err, io.EOF) {\n\t\t\treturn res, nil\n\t\t}\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn res, err\n\t\t}\n\t}\n}\n\n// parseLine decodes one non-blank, whitespace-trimmed line into a Record.\nfunc parseLine(data []byte) (Record, error) {\n\tif data[0] != '{' {\n\t\treturn Record{}, errors.New(\"not a JSON object\")\n\t}\n\tvar w wireLine\n\tif err := json.Unmarshal(data, &w); err != nil Absent fields decode to their zero value.\ntype wireLine struct {\n\tExecutionID string `json:\"execution_id\"`\n\tStartedAt   string `json:\"started_at\"`\n\tStatus      string `json:\"status\"`\n\tJob         struct {\n\t\tCommandName string `json:\"command_name\"`\n\t\tTarget      string `json:\"target\"`\n\t\tLLM         string `json:\"llm\"`\n\t\tModel       string `json:\"model\"`\n\t} `json:\"job\"`\n\tResult struct {\n\t\tReturnCode *int `json:\"returncode\"`\n\t\tTimedOut   bool `json:\"timed_out\"`\n\t\tStats      struct {\n\t\t\tCachedInputTokens int64   `json:\"cached_input_tokens\"`\n\t\t\tElapsedMS         int64   `json:\"elapsed_ms\"`\n\t\t\tInputTokens       int64   `json:\"input_tokens\"`\n\t\t\tOutputTokens      int64   `json:\"output_tokens\"`\n\t\t\tCostUSD           float64 `json:\"cost_usd\"`\n\t\t\tModel             string  `json:\"model\"`\n\t\t} `json:\"stats\"`\n\t} `json:\"result\"`\n}\n\n// Read decodes every non-blank line of r as one log record. Malformed lines are counted in\n// Result.Skipped and described on warn. The error is non-nil only when reading from r fails.\nfunc Read(r io.Reader, warn io.Writer) (Result, error) {\n\tif warn == nil {\n\t\twarn = io.Discard\n\t}\n\tvar res Result\n\tbr := bufio.NewReader(r)\n\tfor lineNo := 1; ; lineNo++ {\n\t\tline, err := br.ReadBytes('\\n')\n\t\tif trimmed := bytes.TrimSpace(line); len(trimmed) > 0 {\n\t\t\trec, perr := parseLine(trimmed)\n\t\t\tif Absent fields decode to their zero value.\ntype wireLine struct {\n\tExecutionID string `json:\"execution_id\"`\n\tStartedAt   string `json:\"started_at\"`\n\tStatus      string `json:\"status\"`\n\tJob         struct {\n\t\tCommandName string `json:\"command_name\"`\n\t\tTarget      string `json:\"target\"`\n\t\tLLM         string `json:\"llm\"`\n\t\tModel       string `json:\"model\"`\n\t} `json:\"job\"`\n\tResult struct {\n\t\tReturnCode *int `json:\"returncode\"`\n\t\tTimedOut   bool `json:\"timed_out\"`\n\t\tStats      struct {\n\t\t\tCachedInputTokens int64   `json:\"cached_input_tokens\"`\n\t\t\tElapsedMS         int64   `json:\"elapsed_ms\"`\n\t\t\tInputTokens       int64   `json:\"input_tokens\"`\n\t\t\tOutputTokens      int64   `json:\"output_tokens\"`\n\t\t\tCostUSD           float64 `json:\"cost_usd\"`\n\t\t\tModel             string  `json:\"model\"`\n\t\t} `json:\"stats\"`\n\t} `json:\"result\"`\n}\n\n// Read decodes every non-blank line of r as one log record. Malformed, incomplete and duplicate\n// lines are counted in Result.Skipped, listed in Result.Skips and described on warn. A duplicate\n// is a later line whose execution_id an earlier kept line already registered. The error is non-nil\n// only when reading from r fails.\nfunc Read(r io.Reader, warn io.Writer) (Result, error) {\n\tif warn == nil {\n\t\twarn = io.Discard\n\t}\n\tvar res Result\n\tseen := make(map[string]bool)\n\tbr := bufio.NewReader(r)\n\tfor lineNo := 1; ; lineNo++ {\n\t\tline, err := br.ReadBytes('\\n')\n\t\tif trimmed := bytes.TrimSpace(line); len(trimmed) > 0 {\n\t\t\trec, perr := parseLine(trimmed)\n\t\t\tif perr == nil && seen[rec.ExecutionID] {\n\t\t\t\tperr = errors.New(\"duplicate execution_id\")\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tif perr != nil {\n\t\t\t\tres.Skipped++\n\t\t\t\tres.Skips = append(res.Skips, Skip{Line: lineNo, Reason: perr.Error()})\n\t\t\t\tfmt.Fprintf(warn, \"line %d: skipped: %v\\n\", lineNo, perr)\n\t\t\t} else {\n\t\t\t\tseen[rec.ExecutionID] = true\n\t\t\t\tres.Records = append(res.Records, rec)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t\tif errors.Is(err, io.EOF) {\n\t\t\treturn res, nil\n\t\t}\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn res, err\n\t\t}\n\t}\n}\n\n// parseLine decodes one non-blank, whitespace-trimmed line into a Record. It fails when the line\n// is not a JSON object or when a required field is absent, empty or not a string.\nfunc parseLine(data []byte) (Record, error) {\n\tif data[0] != '{' {\n\t\treturn Record{}, errors.New(\"not a JSON object\")\n\t}\n\tvar w wireLine\n\tif err := json.Unmarshal(data, &w); err != nil {\n\t\treturn Record{}, err\n\t}\n\trequired := []struct{ name, value string }{\n\t\t{\"execution_id\", w.ExecutionID},\n\t\t{\"started_at\", w.StartedAt},\n\t\t{\"status\", w.Status},\n\t\t{\"job.command_name\", w.Job.CommandName},\n\t\t{\"job.target\", w.Job.Target},\n\t}\n\tfor _, field := range required {\n\t\tif field.value == \"\" {\n\t\t\treturn Record{}, fmt.Errorf(\"missing or empty %s\", field.name)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn Record{\n\t\tExecutionID:    w.ExecutionID,\n\t\tStartedAt:      w.StartedAt,\n\t\tDate:           firstRunes(w.StartedAt, 10),\n\t\tStatus:         w.Status,\n\t\tFailed:         w.Status != \"succeeded\",\n\t\tCommand:        w.Job.CommandName,\n\t\tTarget:         w.Job.Target,\n\t\tProvider:       w.Job.LLM,\n\t\tRequestedModel: w.Job.Model,\n\t\tModel:          w.Result.Stats.Model,\n\t\tInputTokens:    w.Result.Stats.InputTokens,\n\t\tCachedTokens:   w.Result.Stats.CachedInputTokens,\n\t\tOutputTokens:   w.Result.Stats.OutputTokens,\n\t\tCostUSD:        w.Result.Stats.CostUSD,\n\t\tElapsedMS:      w.Result.Stats.ElapsedMS,\n\t\tReturnCode:     w.Result.ReturnCode,\n\t\tTimedOut:       w.Result.TimedOut,\n\t}, nil\n}\n\n// firstRunes returns the first n characters of s, or all of s when it is shorter.\nfunc firstRunes(s string, n int) string {\n\tr := []rune(s)\n\tif len(r) > n {\n\t\tr = r[:n]\n\t}\n\treturn string(r)\n}\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":1,"oldLines":7,"newStart":1,"newLines":7,"lines":[" // Package logread reads Drydock's JSON Lines LLM execution log into Records."," //","-// It never writes to the log it reads.
